/**
Â * [MERGE] Giá»¯ láº¡i hÃ m calculateNodeBox tá»« feature/tt
Â * (ÄÃ¢y lÃ  logic layout/UI)
Â */
function calculateNodeBox(node: NodeData, style: NodeData) {
Â  const { fontSize, nodeLength, nodeText, textCase, shape } = style;
Â  let processedText = nodeText || '';
Â  if (textCase === 'uppercase') processedText = processedText.toUpperCase();
Â  if (textCase === 'lowercase') processedText = processedText.toLowerCase();
Â  const finalFontSize = fontSize || 14;
Â  const finalLineHeight = finalFontSize * LINE_HEIGHT_MULTIPLIER;
Â  let w: number;
Â  let wrappedLines: string[] = [];
Â  const canvas = document.createElement('canvas');
Â  const context = canvas.getContext('2d');
Â  if (context) {
Â  Â  context.font = `${finalFontSize}px ${style.fontFamily || 'Inter'}`;
Â  }
Â  const measureWidth = (text: string) =>
Â  Â  context?.measureText(text).width || text.length * finalFontSize * 0.6;
Â  if (nodeLength === 'fit') {
Â  Â  let maxWidth = 0;
Â  Â  processedText.split('\n').forEach((line: string) => {
Â  Â  Â  maxWidth = Math.max(maxWidth, measureWidth(line));
Â  Â  });
Â  Â  w = maxWidth + PADDING_X * 2;
Â  Â  w = Math.max(w, 80);
Â  Â  wrappedLines = processedText.split('\n');
Â  } else {
Â  Â  w = Number(nodeLength) || 150;
Â  Â  const lines = processedText.split('\n');
Â  Â  lines.forEach((line: string) => {
Â  Â  Â  if (line.length === 0) {
Â  Â  Â  Â  wrappedLines.push('');
Â  Â  Â  Â  return;
Â  Â  Â  }
Â  Â  Â  let currentLine = '';
Â  Â  Â  const words = line.split(' ');
Â  Â  Â  for (const word of words) {
Â  Â  Â  Â  const testLine = currentLine ? `${currentLine} ${word}` : word;
Â  Â  Â  Â  const testWidth = measureWidth(testLine);
Â  Â  Â  Â  if (testWidth > w - PADDING_X * 2) {
Â  Â  Â  Â  Â  if (currentLine) wrappedLines.push(currentLine);
Â  Â  Â  Â  Â  currentLine = word;
Â  Â  Â  Â  Â  while (measureWidth(currentLine) > w - PADDING_X * 2) {
Â  Â  Â  Â  Â  Â  wrappedLines.push(currentLine.substring(0, 20));
Â  Â  Â  Â  Â  Â  currentLine = currentLine.substring(20);
Â  Â  Â  Â  Â  }
Â  Â  Â  Â  } else {
Â  Â  Â  Â  Â  currentLine = testLine;
Â  Â  Â  Â  }
Â  Â  Â  }
Â  Â  Â  if (currentLine) wrappedLines.push(currentLine);
Â  Â  });
Â  }
Â  let h = Math.max(
Â  Â  finalLineHeight + PADDING_Y * 2,
Â  Â  wrappedLines.length * finalLineHeight + PADDING_Y * 2
Â  );
Â  // (Logic shape diamond/ellipse Ä‘Ã£ bá»‹ xÃ³a khá»i feature/tt,
Â  // nhÆ°ng sáº½ Ä‘Æ°á»£c há»— trá»£ bá»Ÿi dataMapper/useEditorStore (GÄ B) náº¿u chÃºng ta render nÃ³)
Â  return { w, h, textToRender: wrappedLines.join('\n'), finalFontSize };
}

Â  Â  Â  Â  const ROOT_X = 0;
Â  Â  Â  Â  const ROOT_Y = 0;
Â  Â  Â  Â  const treeMap = new Map<string, TreeNode>();

Â  Â  Â  Â  const buildTree = (): TreeNode | null => {

Â  Â  Â  Â  Â  Â  treeMap.set(node.id, { ...node, children: [], subtreeHeight: 0 });

Â  Â  Â  Â  Â  edges.forEach((edge) => {

Â  Â  Â  Â  Â  Â  const child = treeMap.get(edge.to);
Â  Â  Â  Â  Â  Â  if (parent && child) {
Â  Â  Â  Â  Â  Â  Â  parent.children.push(child);
Â  Â  Â  Â  Â  Â  }
Â  Â  Â  Â  Â  });
Â  Â  Â  Â  Â  const rootNode = treeMap.get('root');
Â  Â  Â  Â  Â  if (rootNode) root = rootNode;
Â  Â  Â  Â  Â  return root;
Â  Â  Â  Â  };
Â  Â  Â  Â  const calculateSubtreeHeights = (node: TreeNode): number => {
Â  Â  Â  Â  Â  const visual = nodeVisuals.get(node.id);
Â  Â  Â  Â  Â  const selfHeight = visual?.box.h || 60;
Â  Â  Â  Â  Â  if (node.children.length === 0) {
Â  Â  Â  Â  Â  Â  node.subtreeHeight = selfHeight;
Â  Â  Â  Â  Â  Â  return selfHeight;
Â  Â  Â  Â  Â  }
Â  Â  Â  Â  Â  let childrenTotalHeight = 0;
Â  Â  Â  Â  Â  node.children.forEach((child, index) => {
Â  Â  Â  Â  Â  Â  childrenTotalHeight += calculateSubtreeHeights(child);
Â  Â  Â  Â  Â  Â  if (index > 0) {

Â  Â  Â  Â  Â  Â  }
Â  Â  Â  Â  Â  });
Â  Â  Â  Â  Â  node.subtreeHeight = Math.max(selfHeight, childrenTotalHeight);
Â  Â  Â  Â  Â  return node.subtreeHeight;
Â  Â  Â  Â  };
Â  Â  Â  Â  const positionBranch = (
Â  Â  Â  Â  Â  branchNodes: TreeNode[],

Â  Â  Â  Â  ) => {

Â  Â  Â  Â  Â  Â  return sum + node.subtreeHeight + (index > 0 ? VERTICAL_GAP : 0);
Â  Â  Â  Â  Â  }, 0);
Â  Â  Â  Â  Â  let currentY = parent.y - totalHeight / 2;
Â  Â  Â  Â  Â  const direction = side === 'left' ? -1 : 1;

Â  Â  Â  Â  Â  Â  const blockHeight = node.subtreeHeight;
Â  Â  Â  Â  Â  Â  const nodeVisual = nodeVisuals.get(node.id);
Â  Â  Â  Â  Â  Â  const nodeWidth = nodeVisual?.box.w || 150;
  Â  Â  Â  Â  Â  const parentVisual = nodeVisuals.get(parent.id);

Â  Â  Â  Â  Â  Â  Â  parent.x +

Â  Â  Â  Â  Â  Â  Â  positionChildrenVertically(node.children, node, side);
Â  Â  Â  Â  Â  Â  }
Â  Â  Â  Â  Â  });
Â  Â  Â  Â  };
Â  Â  Â  Â  const positionChildrenVertically = (

Â  Â  Â  Â  Â  Â  const blockHeight = node.subtreeHeight;

Â  Â  Â  Â  Â  Â  const nodeWidth = nodeVisual?.box.w || 150;

Â  Â  Â  Â  Â  Â  }
Â  Â  Â  Â  Â  });
Â  Â  Â  Â  };
Â  Â  Â  Â  const rootNode = buildTree();
Â  Â  Â  Â  if (rootNode) {

Â  Â  Â  Â  Â  let leftHeight = 0;

Â  Â  Â  Â  Â  );

Â  Â  Â  Â  Â  Â  } else {

Â  Â  Â  Â  Â  Â  }
Â  Â  Â  Â  Â  });

Â  Â  Â  Â  Â  positionBranch(leftGroup, rootNode, 'left');

Â  Â  Â  Â  Â  Â  const { children, subtreeHeight, ...rest } = node;
Â  Â  Â  Â  Â  Â  return rest;
Â  Â  Â  Â  Â  });
Â  Â  Â  Â  }
Â  Â  Â  } else {
Â  Â  Â  Â  // Logic Dagre cho 'org' vÃ  'logic'
Â  Â  Â  Â  const g = new dagre.graphlib.Graph();
Â  Â  Â  Â  const rankdir = layoutType === 'org' ? 'TB' : 'LR';
Â  Â  Â  Â  g.setGraph({ rankdir: rankdir, nodesep: 50, ranksep: 120 });
Â  Â  Â  Â  g.setDefaultEdgeLabel(() => ({}));
Â  Â  Â  Â  const adjMap = new Map<string, string[]>();
Â  Â  Â  Â  edges.forEach((e) => {

Â  Â  Â  Â  Â  adjMap.get(e.from)!.push(e.to);
Â  Â  Â  Â  });

Â  Â  Â  Â  Â  let h = visual?.box.h || 50;

Â  Â  Â  Â  });

Â  const handleDragMove = (e: any, draggedNodeId: string) => {
Â  Â  const pos = e.target.position();
Â  Â  let targetFound: string | null = null;
Â  Â  for (const node of nodes) {
Â  Â  Â  if (node.id === draggedNodeId) continue;
Â  Â  Â  const visual = nodeVisuals.get(node.id);
Â  Â  Â  if (!visual) continue;
Â  Â  Â  const { w, h } = visual.box;
Â  Â  Â  const { x, y } = visual.style;
Â  Â  Â  const isOver =
Â  Â  Â  Â  pos.x > x - w / 2 &&
Â  Â  Â  Â  pos.x < x + w / 2 &&
Â  Â  Â  Â  pos.y > y - h / 2 &&
Â  Â  Â  Â  pos.y < y + h / 2;
Â  Â  Â  if (isOver) {
Â  Â  Â  Â  targetFound = node.id;
Â  Â  Â  Â  break;
Â  Â  Â  }
Â  Â  }
Â  Â  setDropTargetId(targetFound);
Â  };

Â  const handleDragEnd = (e: any, draggedNodeId: string) => {
Â  Â  setDragStartState(null);
Â  Â  const finalX = e.target.x();
Â  Â  const finalY = e.target.y();

Â  Â  if (dropTargetId && dropTargetId !== draggedNodeId) {
Â  Â  Â  // Reparent
Â  Â  Â  const draggedNode = nodes.find((n) => n.id === draggedNodeId);
Â  Â  Â  if (!draggedNode) return;
Â  Â  Â  let isDroppingOnChild = false;
Â  Â  Â  const checkChildren = (id: string) => {
Â  Â  Â  Â  if (id === dropTargetId) {
Â  Â  Â  Â  Â  isDroppingOnChild = true;
Â  Â  Â  Â  Â  return;
Â  Â  Â  Â  }
Â  Â  Â  Â  edges.filter((e) => e.from === id).forEach((e) => checkChildren(e.to));
Â  Â  Â  };
Â  Â  Â  checkChildren(draggedNodeId);
Â  Â  Â  if (isDroppingOnChild) {
Â  Â  Â  Â  addToast('KhÃ´ng thá»ƒ di chuyá»ƒn node cha vÃ o node con!', 'error');
Â  Â  Â  Â  setGraph(dragStartState?.nodes || nodes, dragStartState?.edges || edges);
Â  Â  Â  Â  setDropTargetId(null);
Â  Â  Â  Â  return;
Â  Â  Â  }
Â  Â  Â  const newParentId = dropTargetId;
Â  Â  Â  const oldEdge = edges.find((e) => e.to === draggedNodeId);
Â  Â  Â  let newEdges: EdgeData[];
Â  Â  Â  if (oldEdge) {
Â  Â  Â  Â  newEdges = edges.map((e) =>
Â  Â  Â  Â  Â  e.id === oldEdge.id ? { ...e, from: newParentId } : e
Â  Â  Â  Â  );
Â  Â  Â  } else {
Â  Â  Â  Â  newEdges = [
Â  Â  Â  Â  Â  ...edges,
Â  Â  Â  Â  Â  { id: `e-${draggedNodeId}`, from: newParentId, to: draggedNodeId },
Â  Â  Â  Â  ];
Â  Â  Â  }
Â  Â  Â  const parentSide = nodeMap.get(newParentId)?.side || 'right';
Â  Â  Â  const newNodes = nodes.map((n) =>
Â  Â  Â  Â  n.id === draggedNodeId
Â  Â  Â  Â  Â  ? {
Â  Â  Â  Â  Â  Â  Â  ...n,
Â  Â  Â  Â  Â  Â  Â  parentId: newParentId,
Â  Â  Â  Â  Â  Â  Â  x: finalX,
Â  Â  Â  Â  Â  Â  Â  y: finalY,
Â  Â  Â  Â  Â  Â  Â  side: parentSide,
Â  Â  Â  Â  Â  Â  }
Â  Â  Â  Â  Â  : n
Â  Â  Â  );
Â  Â  Â  setGraph(newNodes, newEdges);
Â  Â  Â  // [GÄ 9] Gá»­i patch
Â  Â  Â  sendPatch('NODE_REPARENT', {
Â  Â  Â  Â  nodeId: draggedNodeId, newParentId, x: finalX, y: finalY, side: parentSide,
Â  Â  Â  });
Â  Â  } else {
Â  Â  Â  // KÃ©o
Â  Â  Â  const newNodes = nodes.map((n) => {
Â  Â  Â  Â  if (n.id === draggedNodeId) {
Â  Â  Â  Â  Â  if (n.parentId) {
Â  Â  Â  Â  Â  Â  return { ...n, x: finalX, y: finalY };
Â  Â  Â  Â  Â  }
Â  Â  Â  Â  Â  return {
Â  Â  Â  Â  Â  Â  ...n, x: finalX, y: finalY, parentId: undefined, side: undefined,
Â  Â  Â  Â  Â  };
Â  Â  Â  Â  }
Â  Â  Â  Â  return n;
Â  Â  Â  });
Â  Â  Â  setGraph(newNodes, edges);
Â  Â  Â  // [GÄ 9] Gá»­i patch
Â  Â  Â  sendPatch('NODE_MOVE', { id: draggedNodeId, x: finalX, y: finalY });
Â  Â  }

Â  Â  setTimeout(() => handleLayout(true), 50); // Giá»¯ camera
Â  Â  // [GÄ 7] Gá»i debouncer
Â  Â  // [Sá»¬A 2] XÃ³a debouncedPushHistory. ÄÃ£ push á»Ÿ handleDragStart.
Â  Â  // debouncedPushHistory();
Â  Â  debouncedPersistData();

Â  const handleToggleCollapse = useCallback(

Â  // [MERGE] Giá»¯ láº¡i logic UI má»›i (isNodeVisible) tá»« feature/tt
Â  const isNodeVisible = useCallback((nodeId: string): boolean => {
Â  Â  const node = nodeMap.get(nodeId);
Â  Â  if (!node) return false;
Â  Â  if (nodeId === 'root' || !node.parentId) return true;
Â  Â  if (node.parentId === 'root') {
Â  Â  Â  if (node.side === 'left' && rootCollapse.left) return false;
Â  Â  Â  if ((node.side === 'right' || !node.side) && rootCollapse.right) return false;
Â  Â  }
Â  Â  const parent = nodeMap.get(node.parentId);
Â  Â  if (!parent) return true;
Â  Â  if (parent.collapsed) return false;
Â  Â  if (parent.id === 'root') return true;
Â  Â  return isNodeVisible(node.parentId);
Â  }, [nodeMap, rootCollapse]);

Â  const visibleNodes = useMemo(
Â  Â  () => nodes.filter((n) => isNodeVisible(n.id)),
Â  Â  [nodes, isNodeVisible]
Â  );
Â  const visibleNodeIds = useMemo(
Â  Â  () => new Set(visibleNodes.map((n) => n.id)),
Â  Â  [visibleNodes]

Â  Â  () =>

Â  Â  Â  Â  (e) => visibleNodeIds.has(e.from) && visibleNodeIds.has(e.to)
Â  Â  Â  ),
Â  Â  [edges, visibleNodeIds]

Â  // [MERGE] Giá»¯ láº¡i logic UI má»›i (descendantCounts) tá»« feature/tt
Â  const descendantCounts = useMemo(() => {
Â  Â  const counts = new Map<string, number>();
Â  Â  const rootCounts = { left: 0, right: 0 };
Â  Â  const adj = new Map<string, string[]>();
Â  Â  for (const edge of edges) {
Â  Â  Â  if (!adj.has(edge.from)) adj.set(edge.from, []);
Â  Â  Â  adj.get(edge.from)!.push(edge.to);
Â  Â  }
Â  Â  const dfs = (nodeId: string): number => {
Â  Â  Â  const children = adj.get(nodeId) || [];
Â  Â  Â  let total = children.length;
Â  Â  Â  for (const childId of children) {
Â  Â  Â  Â  total += dfs(childId);
Â  Â  Â  }
Â  Â  Â  counts.set(nodeId, total);
Â  Â  Â  return total;
Â  Â  };
Â  Â  for (const nodeId of nodeMap.keys()) {
Â  Â  Â  if (!counts.has(nodeId)) {
Â  Â  Â  Â  dfs(nodeId);
Â  Â  Â  }
Â  Â  }
Â  Â  const rootChildren = adj.get('root') || [];
Â  Â  for (const childId of rootChildren) {
Â  Â  Â  const childNode = nodeMap.get(childId);
Â  Â  Â  const childTotalCount = (counts.get(childId) || 0) + 1;
Â  Â  Â  if (childNode?.side === 'left') {
Â  Â  Â  Â  rootCounts.left += childTotalCount;
Â  Â  Â  } else {
Â  Â  Â  Â  rootCounts.right += childTotalCount;
Â  Â  Â  }
Â  Â  }
Â  Â  return { counts, rootCounts };
Â  }, [edges, nodeMap]);

// src/services/api.ts
import axios, { AxiosHeaders } from "axios";
// Sá»¬A: ThÃªm clearTokens, thay Ä‘á»•i imports
import { loadTokens, saveTokens, clearTokens, type Tokens } from "./authStorage"; 
import { getCurrentUserSession } from "../auth/cognitoDirect"; // Sá»¬A: Import tá»« cognitoDirect

const baseURL =
 import.meta.env.VITE_API_URL?.trim() ||
// ... existing code ...
 "/api";

const api = axios.create({ baseURL });

// Sá»¬A: Cáº­p nháº­t toÃ n bá»™ logic interceptor Ä‘á»ƒ dÃ¹ng SDK
api.interceptors.request.use(async (config) => {
 const t = loadTokens();
 if (!t?.access_token) return config; // KhÃ´ng cÃ³ token, cá»© gá»­i request

 const now = Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000);
 // Kiá»ƒm tra 60 giÃ¢y trÆ°á»›c khi háº¿t háº¡n
 const expiresSoon = (t.expires_at ?? 0) - now <= 60; 

 let accessToken = t.access_token; // Máº·c Ä‘á»‹nh dÃ¹ng token cÅ©

 if (expiresSoon) {
  // Token sáº¯p háº¿t háº¡n, thá»­ lÃ m má»›i báº±ng SDK
  try {
   const session = await getCurrentUserSession(); // SDK sáº½ tá»± Ä‘á»™ng refresh náº¿u cáº§n
   const newAccessToken = session.getAccessToken().getJwtToken();
   const newIdToken = session.getIdToken().getJwtToken();
   const newRefreshToken = session.getRefreshToken()?.getToken() ?? t.refresh_token;

   const next: Tokens = {
    access_token: newAccessToken,
    id_token: newIdToken,
    refresh_token: newRefreshToken,
    expires_at: session.getAccessToken().getExpiration(),
   };
   saveTokens(next); // LÆ°u token má»›i
   accessToken = next.access_token; // DÃ¹ng token má»›i cho request nÃ y
  } catch (e) {
   console.error("API interceptor refresh failed:", e);
   // Refresh tháº¥t báº¡i, xÃ³a token vÃ  táº£i láº¡i trang Ä‘á»ƒ logout
   clearTokens();
   window.location.reload(); 
   return Promise.reject(new Error("Session expired, logging out."));
  }
 }

 // GÃ¡n header Authorization vá»›i token (cÅ© hoáº·c má»›i)
 if (!config.headers) config.headers = new AxiosHeaders();
 (config.headers as AxiosHeaders).set(
  "Authorization",
  `Bearer ${accessToken}`
 );
 return config;
});

export default api;

==== D:\Ver2_MM\mindmap-project copy 2\web\src\services\AuthApi.ts ====
import { cognitoConfig } from '../auth/cognitoConfig';

// Base URL for your custom authentication backend
// [GIá»® NGUYÃŠN] VÃ¬ cÃ¡c hÃ m cÅ© (loginUser, registerUser...) Ä‘ang dÃ¹ng nÃ³.
const API_BASE = 'http://localhost:8081/api/auth';

/**
 * A utility function to parse the response from the fetch API.
 * It handles both JSON and text responses.
 * @param response The fetch response object.
 * @returns A promise that resolves to an object with 'ok' status and 'body'.
 */
export const parseResponse = async (response: Response) => {
  const text = await response.text();
  try {
    return { ok: response.ok, status: response.status, body: JSON.parse(text) };
  } catch {
    return { ok: response.ok, status: response.status, body: text || {} };
  }
};

/**
 * [Má»šI] LÃ m má»›i (refresh) Access Token báº±ng Refresh Token.
 * HÃ m nÃ y gá»i tháº³ng Ä‘áº¿n endpoint /oauth2/token cá»§a Cognito
 * (sá»­ dá»¥ng VITE_COGNITO_DOMAIN, giáº£i quyáº¿t lá»—i xung Ä‘á»™t tÃªn miá»n).
 */
export const refreshToken = async (refreshToken: string) => {
  const body = new URLSearchParams({
    grant_type: 'refresh_token',
    client_id: cognitoConfig.ClientId,
    refresh_token: refreshToken,
  });

  const res = await fetch(`https://${cognitoConfig.Domain}/oauth2/token`, {
    method: 'POST',
    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/x-www-form-urlencoded' },
    body,
  });

  if (!res.ok) {
    const errorBody = await res.json();
    console.error("Token refresh failed:", errorBody);
    throw new Error(`token_refresh_failed: ${errorBody.error_description || res.status}`);
  }

  // Tráº£ vá» token má»›i (Access Token vÃ  ID Token)
  // Cognito KHÃ”NG tráº£ vá» Refresh Token má»›i trong luá»“ng nÃ y
  return res.json() as Promise<{
    access_token: string;
    id_token: string;
    expires_in?: number;
  }>;
};

/**
 * Checks if an email already exists in the system.
 * @param email The email to check.
 * @returns A promise with the result of the check.
 */
export const checkEmail = async (email: string) => {
  const resp = await fetch(`${API_BASE}/checkemail`, {
    method: 'POST',
    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
    body: JSON.stringify({ email }),
  });
  return await parseResponse(resp);
};

/**
 * Logs in a user with email and password.
 * @param email The user's email.
 * @param password The user's password.
 * @returns A promise with the login result, including tokens.
 */
export const loginUser = async (email: string, password: string) => {
  const resp = await fetch(`${API_BASE}/login`, {
    method: 'POST',
    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
    body: JSON.stringify({ email, password }),
  });
  return await parseResponse(resp);
};

/**
 * Registers a new user.
 * @param email The new user's email.
 * @param password The new user's password.
 * @returns A promise with the registration result.
 */
export const registerUser = async (email: string, password: string) => {
  const resp = await fetch(`${API_BASE}/cognito/register`, {
    method: 'POST',
    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
    body: JSON.stringify({ email, password }),
  });
  return await parseResponse(resp);
};

/**
 * Confirms a user's registration with a confirmation code.
 * @param username The username (email) to confirm.
 * @param code The confirmation code sent to the user.
 * @returns A promise with the confirmation result.
 */
export const confirmSignup = async (username: string, code: string) => {
  const resp = await fetch(`${API_BASE}/cognito/confirm`, {
    method: 'POST',
    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
    body: JSON.stringify({ username, code }),
  });
  return await parseResponse(resp);
};

/**
 * Resends the confirmation code to a user.
 * @param username The username (email) to resend the code to.
 * @returns A promise with the result of the resend operation.
 */
export const resendConfirmationCode = async (username: string) => {
  const resp = await fetch(`${API_BASE}/cognito/resend`, {
    method: 'POST',
    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
    body: JSON.stringify({ username }),
  });
  return await parseResponse(resp);
};


/**
 * Initiates the forgot password flow for a user.
 * @param username The user's email.
 * @returns A promise with the result.
 */
export const startForgotPassword = async (username: string) => {
    const resp = await fetch(`${API_BASE}/forgot`, {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ username }),
    });
    return await parseResponse(resp);
};

/**
 * Confirms the password reset with a code and new password.
 * @param username The user's email.
 * @param code The reset code sent to the user.
 * @param newPassword The new password.
 * @returns A promise with the result of the reset operation.
 */
export const confirmResetPassword = async (username: string, code: string, newPassword: string) => {
    const resp = await fetch(`${API_BASE}/reset`, {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ username, code, newPassword }),
    });
    return await parseResponse(resp);
};


/**
 * Generates the URL for Google login via Cognito Hosted UI.
 * (HÃ m nÃ y váº«n chÃ­nh xÃ¡c)
 */
export const getGoogleLoginUrl = () => {
  const params = new URLSearchParams({
    response_type: 'code',
    client_id: cognitoConfig.ClientId,
    redirect_uri: cognitoConfig.RedirectUri,
    scope: cognitoConfig.Scope,
    identity_provider: 'Google',
  });
  return `https://${cognitoConfig.Domain}/oauth2/authorize?${params.toString()}`;
};

==== D:\Ver2_MM\mindmap-project copy 2\web\src\services\authStorage.ts ====
// src/services/authStorage.ts
export type Tokens = {
  access_token: string;
  refresh_token?: string;
  id_token?: string;
  expires_at?: number; // epoch seconds
};

const KEY = "mm_tokens";

export function loadTokens(): Tokens | null {
  try { return JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(KEY) || "null"); }
  catch { return null; }
}

export function saveTokens(t: Tokens) {
  localStorage.setItem(KEY, JSON.stringify(t));
}

export function clearTokens() {
  localStorage.removeItem(KEY);
}

export function isExpired(t?: Tokens | null, skewSeconds = 30): boolean {
  if (!t?.expires_at) return true;
  return t.expires_at <= Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000) + skewSeconds;
}

==== D:\Ver2_MM\mindmap-project copy 2\web\src\services\dataMapper.ts ====
/**
 * Lá»šP PHIÃŠN Dá»ŠCH Dá»® LIá»†U (DATA MAPPER)
 *
 * [Cáº¬P NHáº¬T GIAI ÄOáº N B]
 * - ÄÃ£ thÃªm `styleLocked` vÃ o `BeNodeStyle`.
 * - Cáº­p nháº­t cÃ¡c hÃ m `normalizeNodeFEtoBE` vÃ  `normalizeNodeBEtoFE`
 * Ä‘á»ƒ "dá»‹ch" trÆ°á»ng `styleLocked` má»›i tá»« `useEditorStore`.
 * - Logic Di cÆ° (Migration) (GÄ 4) váº«n Ä‘Æ°á»£c giá»¯ nguyÃªn.
 */

// 1. IMPORT TYPES Cá»¦A FRONTEND
import {
  NodeData as FeNodeData,
  EdgeData as FeEdgeData,
} from '../app/store/useEditorStore'; // Import tá»« store Ä‘Ã£ cáº­p nháº­t (GÄ B)

// =================================================================================
// 2. Äá»ŠNH NGHÄ¨A TYPES (INTERFACE) Cá»¦A BACKEND
// =================================================================================

export type BeNodeStyle = {
  // === CÃ¡c trÆ°á»ng BE gá»‘c ===
  shape: 'rectangle' | 'roundedRect' | 'diamond' | 'ellipse'; // Äáº£m báº£o há»— trá»£ Ä‘áº§y Ä‘á»§
  backgroundColor: string; // TÆ°Æ¡ng á»©ng 'color' cá»§a FE
  textColor: string;
  borderStyle: 'solid' | 'dashed' | 'dotted';
  fontWeight: 'normal' | 'bold';
  fontStyle: 'normal' | 'italic';

  // === CÃ¡c trÆ°á»ng FE Ä‘Æ°á»£c "lá»“ng" vÃ o Ä‘Ã¢y ===
  borderColor?: string;
  borderWidth?: number;
  fontFamily?: string;
  fontSize?: number;
  textDecoration?: 'none' | 'underline' | 'line-through';
  textAlign?: 'left' | 'center' | 'right';
  textCase?: 'normal' | 'uppercase' | 'lowercase';
  nodeLength?: 'fit' | number;
  quickStyleId?: string;
  localStructure?: 'default' | 'logic' | 'org';
  branchColor?: string;
  branchLineStyle?: 'bezier' | 'sharp';
  branchLineEnd?: 'none' | 'arrow';
  branchLineThickness?: 'thin' | 'normal' | 'thick';

  // [Má»šI GÄ B] ThÃªm trÆ°á»ng 'styleLocked' tá»« feature/tt
  styleLocked?: boolean;
};

export type BeNodeData = {
  id: string;
  text: string;
  x: number;
  y: number;
  parentId: string | null;
  collapsed: boolean;
  style: Partial<BeNodeStyle>;
  side?: 'left' | 'right';
  hyperlink?: string | null;
  notes?: string | null;
  externalReference?: any | null;
};

export type BeEdgeData = {
  id: string;
  from: string;
  to: string;
  style?: any;
};

export type BeMindmapContent = {
  layoutMode: string;
  theme: string;
  nodes: BeNodeData[];
  edges: BeEdgeData[];
};

export type BeMindmapDoc = {
  id: string;

    isPublic: boolean;

  };

  updatedAt: string;
  version: number;
  isMigratedGuestMap?: boolean;
};

// =================================================================================
// 3. VIáº¾T HÃ€M PHIÃŠN Dá»ŠCH (NORMALIZATION) - (Cáº­p nháº­t GÄ B)
// =================================================================================

/**
 * Dá»‹ch 1 Node: Frontend (pháº³ng) -> Backend (lá»“ng style)
 * [Cáº¬P NHáº¬T GÄ B]: ThÃªm `styleLocked`
 */
export function normalizeNodeFEtoBE(feNode: FeNodeData): BeNodeData {
  const {
    // 1. Thuá»™c tÃ­nh gá»‘c cá»§a BE
    id,
    nodeText, // Äá»•i tÃªn
    x,
    y,
    parentId,
    collapsed,
    side,

    // 2. Thuá»™c tÃ­nh sáº½ Ä‘Æ°á»£c lá»“ng vÃ o 'style'
    shape,
    color, // Äá»•i tÃªn
    borderColor,
    borderWidth,
    borderStyle,
    fontFamily,
    fontSize,
    fontWeight,
    fontStyle,
    textDecoration,
    textAlign,
    textColor,
    textCase,
    nodeLength,
    localStructure,
    branchColor,
    branchLineStyle,
    branchLineEnd,
    branchLineThickness,
    quickStyleId,

    // [Má»šI GÄ B] ThÃªm trÆ°á»ng 'styleLocked'
    styleLocked,
  } = feNode;

  // Táº¡o object 'style' lá»“ng nhau
  const beStyle: Partial<BeNodeStyle> = {
    backgroundColor: color,
    textColor: textColor,
    shape: shape,
    borderColor,
    borderWidth,
    borderStyle,
    fontFamily,
    fontSize,
    fontWeight,
    fontStyle,
    textDecoration,
    textAlign,
    textCase,
    nodeLength,
    localStructure,
    branchColor,
    branchLineStyle,
    branchLineEnd,
    branchLineThickness,
    quickStyleId: quickStyleId,
    styleLocked: styleLocked, // [Má»šI GÄ B]
  };

  // Táº¡o object NodeData cá»§a BE
  const beNode: BeNodeData = {
    id,
    text: nodeText,
    x,
    y,
    parentId: parentId || null,
    collapsed: collapsed || false,
    side: side,
    style: beStyle,
  };

  return beNode;
}

/**
 * Dá»‹ch 1 Node: Backend (lá»“ng style) -> Frontend (pháº³ng)
 * [Cáº¬P NHáº¬T GÄ B]: ThÃªm `styleLocked`
 */
export function normalizeNodeBEtoFE(beNode: BeNodeData): FeNodeData {
  const { id, text, x, y, parentId, collapsed, side, style } = beNode;

  // [FIX] ThÃªm kiá»ƒm tra 'style' null (tá»« GÄ 5)
  const safeStyle = style || {};

  // "LÃ m pháº³ng" (flatten) object 'style'
  const feNode: FeNodeData = {
    id,
    nodeText: text,
    x,
    y,
    parentId: parentId || undefined,
    collapsed: collapsed || false,
    side: side,

    // Tráº£i pháº³ng cÃ¡c thuá»™c tÃ­nh tá»« 'style'
    shape: safeStyle.shape,
    color: safeStyle.backgroundColor,
    borderColor: safeStyle.borderColor,
    borderWidth: safeStyle.borderWidth,
    borderStyle: safeStyle.borderStyle,
    fontFamily: safeStyle.fontFamily,
    fontSize: safeStyle.fontSize,
    fontWeight: safeStyle.fontWeight,
    fontStyle: safeStyle.fontStyle,
    textDecoration: safeStyle.textDecoration,
    textAlign: safeStyle.textAlign,
    textColor: safeStyle.textColor,
    textCase: safeStyle.textCase,
    nodeLength: safeStyle.nodeLength,
    localStructure: safeStyle.localStructure,
    branchColor: safeStyle.branchColor,
    branchLineStyle: safeStyle.branchLineStyle,
    branchLineEnd: safeStyle.branchLineEnd,
    branchLineThickness: safeStyle.branchLineThickness,
    quickStyleId: safeStyle.quickStyleId as any,
    styleLocked: safeStyle.styleLocked, // [Má»šI GÄ B]
  };

  return feNode;
}

// =================================================================================
// 4. VIáº¾T HÃ€M PHIÃŠN Dá»ŠCH TOÃ€N Bá»˜ CONTENT - (Giai Ä‘oáº¡n 1 & 2)
// (KhÃ´ng thay Ä‘á»•i trong GÄ B, vÃ¬ cÃ¡c hÃ m con Ä‘Ã£ Ä‘Æ°á»£c cáº­p nháº­t)
// =================================================================================

export function normalizeContentFEtoBE(
  feNodes: FeNodeData[],
  feEdges: FeEdgeData[],
  globalSettings: { layoutMode?: string; theme?: string } = {}
): BeMindmapContent {
  const beNodes = feNodes.map(normalizeNodeFEtoBE); // HÃ m nÃ y Ä‘Ã£ Ä‘Æ°á»£c cáº­p nháº­t

  return {
    layoutMode: globalSettings.layoutMode || 'mindmap',
    theme: globalSettings.theme || 'light',
    nodes: beNodes,
    edges: feEdges.map((edge) => ({ ...edge })),
  };
}

export function normalizeContentBEtoFE(
  beContent: BeMindmapContent | undefined | null
): { nodes: FeNodeData[]; edges: FeEdgeData[]; layoutMode: string; theme: string } {
  if (!beContent || !beContent.nodes || beContent.nodes.length === 0) {
    const rootNode = normalizeNodeBEtoFE({
      id: 'root',
      text: 'Chá»§ Ä‘á» chÃ­nh',
      x: 0,
      y: 0,
      collapsed: false,
      parentId: null,
      side: 'right',
      style: {
        shape: 'roundedRect',
        backgroundColor: '#FFFFFF',
        textColor: '#1E293B',
        borderStyle: 'solid',
        fontWeight: 'normal',
        fontStyle: 'normal',
      },
    });
    return {
      nodes: [rootNode],
      edges: [],
      layoutMode: 'mindmap',
      theme: 'light',
    };
  }

  const feNodes = beContent.nodes.map(normalizeNodeBEtoFE); // HÃ m nÃ y Ä‘Ã£ Ä‘Æ°á»£c cáº­p nháº­t

  return {
    nodes: feNodes,
    edges: beContent.edges.map((edge) => ({
      id: edge.id,
      from: edge.from,
      to: edge.to,
    })),
    layoutMode: beContent.layoutMode || 'mindmap',
    theme: beContent.theme || 'light',
  };
}

// =================================================================================
// 5. GUEST DATA MIGRATION (Giai Ä‘oáº¡n 4)
// (KhÃ´ng thay Ä‘á»•i trong GÄ B, vÃ¬ cÃ¡c hÃ m con Ä‘Ã£ Ä‘Æ°á»£c cáº­p nháº­t)
// =================================================================================

// Äá»‹nh nghÄ©a cáº¥u trÃºc (cÅ©) cá»§a Guest Doc trong localStorage
type OldFeGuestMapItem = {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  createdAt: string;
};
type OldFeGuestDoc = {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  content: {
    nodes: { [key: string]: any }; // Dáº¡ng Map
    edges: FeEdgeData[];
  };
};

export function migrateOldGuestDataToBE(
  guestMapsRaw: string,
  guestDocsRaw: string
): BeMindmapDoc[] {
  let guestList: OldFeGuestMapItem[] = [];
  let guestDocs: { [id: string]: OldFeGuestDoc } = {};

  try {
    guestList = JSON.parse(guestMapsRaw || '[]');
    guestDocs = JSON.parse(guestDocsRaw || '{}');
  } catch (e) {
    console.error('Lá»—i parse dá»¯ liá»‡u Guest cÅ©:', e);
    return [];
  }

  const migratedDocs: BeMindmapDoc[] = [];

  for (const guestItem of guestList) {
    const oldDoc = guestDocs[guestItem.id];
    if (!oldDoc || !oldDoc.content) {
      continue;
    }

    const oldNodesList: any[] = Object.values(oldDoc.content.nodes || {});
    const oldEdgesList: FeEdgeData[] = oldDoc.content.edges || [];

    const beNodes: BeNodeData[] = oldNodesList.map((oldNode) => {
      // 1. Táº¡o má»™t Ä‘á»‘i tÆ°á»£ng FeNodeData (pháº³ng) táº¡m thá»i
      const tempFeNode: FeNodeData = {
        id: oldNode.id,
        nodeText: oldNode.nodeText ?? oldNode.text ?? '',
        x: oldNode.x || 0,
        y: oldNode.y || 0,
        parentId: oldNode.parentId,
        collapsed: oldNode.collapsed || false,
        side: oldNode.side,
        shape: oldNode.shape,
        color: oldNode.color ?? oldNode.fill,
        borderColor: oldNode.borderColor ?? oldNode.stroke,
        borderWidth: oldNode.borderWidth,
        borderStyle: oldNode.borderStyle,
        fontFamily: oldNode.fontFamily,
        fontSize: oldNode.fontSize,
        fontWeight: oldNode.fontWeight,
        fontStyle: oldNode.fontStyle,
        textDecoration: oldNode.textDecoration,
        textAlign: oldNode.textAlign,
        textColor: oldNode.textColor,
        textCase: oldNode.textCase,
        nodeLength: oldNode.nodeLength,
        localStructure: oldNode.localStructure,
        branchColor: oldNode.branchColor,
        branchLineStyle: oldNode.branchLineStyle,
        branchLineEnd: oldNode.branchLineEnd,
        branchLineThickness: oldNode.branchLineThickness,
        quickStyleId: oldNode.quickStyleId,
        styleLocked: oldNode.styleLocked, // [Má»šI GÄ B] Di cÆ° trÆ°á»ng má»›i
      };

      // 2. TÃ¡i sá»­ dá»¥ng hÃ m chuáº©n hÃ³a
      return normalizeNodeFEtoBE(tempFeNode);
    });

    // 3. Táº¡o BeMindmapContent
    const beContent: BeMindmapContent = {
      layoutMode: 'mindmap',
      theme: 'light',
      nodes: beNodes,
      edges: oldEdgesList.map((e) => ({ id: e.id, from: e.from, to: e.to })),
    };

    // 4. Táº¡o BeMindmapDoc hoÃ n chá»‰nh
    const beDoc: BeMindmapDoc = {
      id: guestItem.id,
      name: guestItem.name,
      content: beContent,
      createdAt: guestItem.createdAt,
      updatedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
      isMigratedGuestMap: true,
      ownerId: '',
      lastEditedBy: '',
      version: 0,
      tags: [],
      accessSettings: { isPublic: false, publicAccessLevel: 'DISABLED' },
    };

    migratedDocs.push(beDoc);
  }

  return migratedDocs;
}

==== D:\Ver2_MM\mindmap-project copy 2\web\src\services\mindmapsApi.ts ====
/**
 * Lá»šP GIAO TIáº¾P API (Giai Ä‘oáº¡n 6: Tá»‘i Æ°u List/Map)
 *
 * Tá»‡p nÃ y Ä‘Ã£ Ä‘Æ°á»£c "dáº¡y" Ä‘á»ƒ sá»­ dá»¥ng Data Mapper.
 * - Khi NHáº¬N (GET), nÃ³ dá»‹ch dá»¯ liá»‡u BE (lá»“ng) sang FE (pháº³ng).
 * - Khi Gá»¬I (POST/PUT), nÃ³ dá»‹ch dá»¯ liá»‡u FE (pháº³ng) sang BE (lá»“ng).
 *
 * [Cáº¬P NHáº¬T GÄ 6]: HÃ m `update` Ä‘Ã£ Ä‘Æ°á»£c sá»­a Ä‘á»ƒ cháº¥p nháº­n `nodes`
 * lÃ  má»™t Máº£ng (List) thay vÃ¬ Map, loáº¡i bá» chuyá»ƒn Ä‘á»•i
 * `Object.values()` khÃ´ng cáº§n thiáº¿t.
 */

import api from './api';

// === CÃC IMPORT (GÄ 1-4) ===
import {
  // Types (Giao diá»‡n) cá»§a Backend
  BeMindmapDoc,
  BeMindmapContent,

  // HÃ m "PhiÃªn dá»‹ch"
  normalizeContentBEtoFE,
  normalizeContentFEtoBE,
  migrateOldGuestDataToBE, // Import tá»« GÄ 4
} from './dataMapper';
import {
  NodeData as FeNodeData,
  EdgeData as FeEdgeData,
} from '../app/store/useEditorStore';
// === Káº¾T THÃšC IMPORT ===

/**
 * [KHÃ”NG Äá»”I] Äá»‹nh nghÄ©a cáº¥u trÃºc dá»¯ liá»‡u pháº³ng
 * mÃ  `Editor.tsx` mong Ä‘á»£i nháº­n Ä‘Æ°á»£c tá»« hÃ m `get()`.
 */
export type FeMindmapDoc = {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  ownerId: string;
  createdAt: string;
  updatedAt: string;
  version: number;
  // ... (cÃ¡c trÆ°á»ng metadata khÃ¡c tá»« BeMindmapDoc náº¿u cáº§n) ...

  // === Ná»™i dung Ä‘Ã£ Ä‘Æ°á»£c "lÃ m pháº³ng" ===
  nodes: FeNodeData[];
  edges: FeEdgeData[];
  layoutMode: string;
  theme: string;
};

/**
 * [KHÃ”NG Äá»”I] Äá»‹nh nghÄ©a cáº¥u trÃºc tÃ³m táº¯t
 * mÃ  `Dashboard.tsx` / `Sidebar.tsx` mong Ä‘á»£i tá»« `list()`.
 */
export type MindmapSummaryDto = {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  createdAt: string;
  updatedAt?: string;
};

// =========================================================================
// mindmapsApi
// =========================================================================
export const mindmapsApi = {
  // ===== Core CRUD =====

  /**
   * [KHÃ”NG Äá»”I] Láº¥y danh sÃ¡ch tÃ³m táº¯t.
   */
  list: async () => (await api.get<MindmapSummaryDto[]>('/mindmaps')).data,

  /**
   * [KHÃ”NG Äá»”I] Láº¥y chi tiáº¿t 1 mindmap.
   */
  get: async (id: string): Promise<FeMindmapDoc> => {
    // 1. Gá»i API vÃ  nháº­n vá» dá»¯ liá»‡u chuáº©n BE (lá»“ng)
    const response = await api.get<BeMindmapDoc>(`/mindmaps/${id}`);
    const beDoc = response.data;

    // 2. "Dá»‹ch" trÆ°á»ng 'content' tá»« BE sang FE
    const feContent = normalizeContentBEtoFE(beDoc.content);

    // 3. Tráº£ vá» má»™t object "pháº³ng" mÃ  Editor.tsx mong Ä‘á»£i
    return {
      id: beDoc.id,
      name: beDoc.name,
      ownerId: beDoc.ownerId,
      createdAt: beDoc.createdAt,
      updatedAt: beDoc.updatedAt,
      version: beDoc.version,

      // GÃ¡n content Ä‘Ã£ Ä‘Æ°á»£c dá»‹ch vÃ  lÃ m pháº³ng
      nodes: feContent.nodes,
      edges: feContent.edges,
      layoutMode: feContent.layoutMode,
      theme: feContent.theme,
    };
  },

  /**
   * [KHÃ”NG Äá»”I] Táº¡o má»›i 1 mindmap.
   */
  create: async (payload: {
    name: string;
    content: { nodes: FeNodeData[]; edges: FeEdgeData[] }; // Nháº­n List FE
  }) => {
    // 1. "Dá»‹ch" content FE sang BE (lá»“ng)
    const beContent = normalizeContentFEtoBE(
      payload.content.nodes,
      payload.content.edges,
      { layoutMode: 'mindmap', theme: 'light' }
    );

    // 2. Gá»­i payload Ä‘Ã£ chuáº©n hÃ³a BE
    const response = await api.post<BeMindmapDoc>('/mindmaps', {
      name: payload.name,
      content: beContent,
    });

    return response.data; // Tráº£ vá» BeMindmapDoc
  },

  /**
   * [ÄÃƒ Cáº¬P NHáº¬T GÄ 6] Cáº­p nháº­t 1 mindmap (dÃ¹ng cho Auto-Save).
   * Dá»‹ch FE (pháº³ng, List) -> BE (lá»“ng, List).
   */
  update: async (
    id: string,
    // [FIX GÄ 6] Signature Ä‘Ã£ Ä‘á»•i
    // Cháº¥p nháº­n `nodes` lÃ  má»™t Máº£ng (List) thay vÃ¬ Map
    payload: {
      name: string;
      content: { nodes: FeNodeData[]; edges: FeEdgeData[] };
    }
  ) => {
    // 1. [FIX GÄ 6] KhÃ´ng cáº§n `Object.values` ná»¯a.
    // Láº¥y tháº³ng Máº£ng (List) tá»« payload.
    const feNodes: FeNodeData[] = payload.content.nodes || [];
    const feEdges: FeEdgeData[] = payload.content.edges || [];

    // 2. "Dá»‹ch" content FE (List) sang BE (List lá»“ng)
    const beContent = normalizeContentFEtoBE(feNodes, feEdges);

    // 3. Gá»­i payload Ä‘Ã£ chuáº©n hÃ³a BE (chá»‰ 'name' vÃ  'content')
    const response = await api.put<BeMindmapDoc>(`/mindmaps/${id}`, {
      name: payload.name,
      content: beContent,
    });

    return response.data; // Tráº£ vá» BeMindmapDoc
  },

  /**
   * [KHÃ”NG Äá»”I] XÃ³a 1 mindmap.
   */
  remove: async (id: string) => (await api.delete<void>(`/mindmaps/${id}`)).data,

  // ===== Aliases (CÃ¡c hÃ m tiá»‡n Ã­ch) =====

  /**
   * [KHÃ”NG Äá»”I] Cáº­p nháº­t tÃªn (DÃ¹ng cho Sidebar).
   */
  updateName: async (id: string, name: string) =>
    (await api.put<BeMindmapDoc>(`/mindmaps/${id}`, { name })).data,

  /**
   * [KHÃ”NG Äá»”I] Alias cho `remove`.
   */
  delete: async (id: string) => (await api.delete<void>(`/mindmaps/${id}`)).data,

  // ===== Guest â†’ Server Sync =====

  /**
   * [KHÃ”NG Äá»”I] Äá»“ng bá»™ Guest (GÄ 4).
   */
  syncGuest: async (migratedDocs: BeMindmapDoc[]) =>
    (await api.post<MindmapSummaryDto[]>('/mindmaps/sync', migratedDocs)).data,

  /**
   * [KHÃ”NG Äá»”I] Táº¡o vÃ  Má»Ÿ (GÄ 2).
   */
  createAndOpen: async () => {
    const feRootNode: FeNodeData = {
      id: 'root',
      nodeText: 'Chá»§ Ä‘á» chÃ­nh',
      x: 0,
      y: 0,
      shape: 'roundedRect',
      color: '#FFFFFF',
      textColor: '#4A5568',
      borderColor: '#CBD5E0',
      borderWidth: 2,
      borderStyle: 'solid',
      fontSize: 14,
      fontWeight: 'normal',
      fontStyle: 'normal',
      textDecoration: 'none',
      textAlign: 'center',
      textCase: 'normal',
      nodeLength: 'fit',
      localStructure: 'default',
      branchLineStyle: 'bezier',
      branchLineEnd: 'none',
      branchLineThickness: 'normal',
      quickStyleId: 'default',
    };

    return mindmapsApi.create({
      name: 'Mindmap má»›i',
      content: {
        nodes: [feRootNode], // Gá»­i Máº¢NG FE
        edges: [],
      },
    });
  },
};

/// <reference types="vite/client" />

interface ImportMetaEnv {
  readonly VITE_COGNITO_DOMAIN: string;
  readonly VITE_COGNITO_CLIENT_ID: string;
  readonly VITE_COGNITO_REDIRECT_URI: string;
  readonly VITE_LOGOUT_REDIRECT_URI: string;
  readonly VITE_API_URL: string;
  readonly VITE_COGNITO_USER_POOL_ID: string;
}

interface ImportMeta {
  readonly env: ImportMetaEnv;
}
